     Tamil Nadu Climate Resilient Urban Development Program assisted by World


 Notes forming part of Program Financial Statement for the period from 06th October 2022 to

1. Background
The World Bank assisted Tamil Nadu Sustainable Urban Development Project (TNSUD
taken up to improve urban services in the participating urban local bodies in a financially
sustainable manner and to pilot improved urban management practices in select citie
which administrative sanction was accorded vide Government order G.O.(Ms.)No.5
Municipal Administration and Water Supply Department, dated 13.01.2016. The tot
of the project is US $ 600 million equivalent to about Rs.3,831 crore, of which World B
assistance is Us $ 400 miltion equivalent to about Rs.2,554 crore.
The Government of Tamil Nadu have accorded in-principle approval vide Governmen
Order first read above to initiate the preparations for seeking Additional Financing ass
Under Tamil Nadu Sustainable Urban Development Project with an outlay of USD 45
[World Bank's additional financing of UsD 300 milion and counterpart financing from
Government of Tamil Nadu of USD 150 million]. The name of the project has
changed from Tamil Nadu Sustainable Urban Development Project-Additional Finan
(TNSUDP-AF) to Tamil Nadu Climate Resilient Urban Development Program (TNC
Further the Government of Tami! Nadu opted to avail the financial assistance from
Bank in Japanese Yen (JPY).
The total outlay. of Tamil Nadu Climate Resilient Urban Development Program is Rs.3,838.65
Crore (equivalent to JPY 69.6797 bilion), of which Worid Bank financing is Rs
(equivalent to JPY 45.2056 billion) and counterpart funding is Rs.1,348.27 cror
JPY 24.4741 bilion) from Government of India, Government of Tamil! Nadu and
The following table specifies each category of withdrawal of the proceeds of the
World Bank (including the Disbursement Linked Indicators as applicable) ("Cat
allocation of the amounts of the Loan to each Category and counterpart funding:

2. Accounting Policies:
a) Basis of Accounting
   The consolidated program financial statements are prepared under the historic
   convention in accordance with generaily accepted accounting principles, on cash
   basis.

    b) Purpose

   The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank f
   TNCRUDP using the Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Prog
    strengthen urban management and improve access to climate resilient urban services in
   Water Supply and Sanitation sector in participating ULBs in Tamil Nadu. This
   consolidated program financial statement prepared based on the expenditure r
    in the Sub-project Audit report.

    c) Savings Bank Interest
   Interest earned on the term deposits/savings bank deposit is recognized on rece

    d) Fixed Assets
   The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at co
   capitalized in the General A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of pur
   price (net of rebates and discounts) duties, levies (net of duty drawbacks and subsidies)
   and any attributable cost of bringing the assets to their working condition. Since th
   work under this project is on-going. the same was accounted under Capital Wo
   progress in the respective ULBs.
3. Notes to Accounts

   a) Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.2025
   aggregating to Rs. 27.10,232/- is to be deposited in State Government fund.

   b) Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reciassified wherever nece
   c) The program reporting requirements are outlined in the Loan Agreement and
   Operation Agreement. The financial reporting requirements including audi
   outlined in the DFIL dated 01.03.2024

   d) As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconciled to the overail
   disbursements from the IBRD and adjustments, if any wil be carried out in the l
   the Program.

   e) During the period upto 31.03.2025 the overall Program expenditure was R
   Crores(excluding PMC Payments), which is more than the disbursement of Rs.105
   Crore) for achievements of DLIs.                                                    Ass

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTE
 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank fin
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to stren
  to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation secto
  This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Ba
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historica
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Progra
  of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates an
  drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the ass
  work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Capital W

  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are fllowing a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.20
  deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconciled
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO AC

 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
    A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank financed P
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to strength
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sect
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in a
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read wi
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical cost co
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.
  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at cost but
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates
  duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the ass
  the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Capita
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed ass
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.
  deposited in State Government fund.

2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever neces
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconiled
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES
1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank
  the Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to streng
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitatio
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared
  reporting provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the W
  Agreement dated 04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historic
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates
  of duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the as
  Since the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under

  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 3 1.03
  be deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever nec
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overll program expenditure will be reconiled
  IBRD and adjustments, if any will be cried out in the last year of the Progra

      The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical cos
      generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

      C. FIXED ASSETS

      drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the assets t
      under this project is on-going. the same was acounted under Capital Work in
      D. DEPRECIATION

      Sstatements, no depreciation is charged.

1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of TamilNadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Ba
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to strengthen u
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitatio
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

   B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historica
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generaly taken under revenue head for the Prog
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates a
  duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the a
  the work under this project is on-going. the same was accounted under Cap
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixe
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2.3 As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconciled to the ov

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO A
 I,   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
      A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
      The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank finan
      the Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to st
      access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sector
      Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in a
      reporting provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read with the
      Agreement dated 04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

      B. CONVENTION
      The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the histo
      generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

      C. FIXED ASSETS
      The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at
      A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of reba
      of duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the
      Since the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted un
      D. DEPRECIATION
      The entities are fllowing a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed
      statements, no depreciation is charged.

2,    NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
 2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 3 1
      deposited in State Government fund.

 2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever nece
 2.3 As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconciled t
      IBRD and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO AC
 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank financed
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to strengthen ur
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sector
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in acco
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the Worid Bank rea
  dated 04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical c
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates an
  duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the assets
  the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Cap

  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed assets. F
  Statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTE TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.0
  deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever necessary.
2.2 As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconciled to the o
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

 SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO
 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank finan
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to streng
  to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sec
  is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in accoradanc
  Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read with the
  and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the histo
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Prog
  of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates a
  drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the asets
  under this project is on-going. the same was accounted under Capital Work in progre

  D. DEPRECIATION

  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed as
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.
  deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever nece
2.3 As tis is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconil
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO ACCOUNTS

 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the Worid Bank financed Pro
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to strengthen urban
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sector in pa
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in accoradance
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read with
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.


  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical cost c
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at cost but capitalized in the G
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates and d
  duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the assets to thei
  the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Capital Wo
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed assets. Fo
  statements, no depreciation is charged.


2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.2025 ag
  deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever necessary
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconciled to the ov
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES T
1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
      A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
     The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the Worid Bank fi
     the Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to s
     access to climate resiient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation
     Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepare
     provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Ba
     dated 04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

     B. CONVENTION
     The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the hist
     generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

     C. FIXED ASSETS
     The Fixed Assets are generlly taken under revenue head for the Progra
     A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebate
     of duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing th
     Since the work under this project is on-going, the same was accoun
     D. DEPRECIATION
     The entities are folowing a policy of charging depreciation on the fi
     statements, no depreciation is charged.


2.   NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interet received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 3
     be deposited in State Government fund.

2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever n
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overll program expenditure will be reconciled to t
     IBRD and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
     A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
     The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Ban
     the Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to stre
     access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation
     Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared
     reporing provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of
     Agreement dated 04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

     B. CONVENTION
     The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historic
     generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

     C. FIXED ASSETS
     The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Prog
     A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of reb
     of duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any atributable cost of bringing
     Since the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted u
     D. DEPRECIATION
     The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixe
     statements, no depreciation is charged.

 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank fin
  Program-for-Resulis (PforR). The objective of the Program is to streng
  to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation se
  is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in accorad
  Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the Worid Bank read wit
  and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historic
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generaly taken under revenue head for the Program
  of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates a
  drawbacks and subsidies) and any atributable cost of bringing the ass
  under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Capital Work in
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are folowing a policy of charging depreciation on the fix
  Statements, no depreciation is charged.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO ACC
 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES

   A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the Worid Bank fi
  the Program-forResuls (PforR). The objective of the Program is to str
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sec
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in acco
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read
  dated 04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.


   B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical co
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.
  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generlly taken under revenue head for the Program a
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates an
  of duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the asse
  Since the work under this project is on-going the same was accounted unde
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed ass
  Statements, no depreciation is charged.

  IBRD and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO ACCOU

 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
     A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
     The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank financed Pro
     Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to strengthen u
     access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sector
     Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in acco
     provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read with
     04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

     B. CONVENTION
     The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical cost con
     generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

     C. FIXED ASSETS
     The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at cost but cap
     A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates
     duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the asse
     the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Capital
     D. DEPRECIATION
     The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed assets. For the
     Statements, no depreciation is charged.

2.   -NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.2025
     deposited in State Government fund.

2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclssified wherever nece
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overal program expenditure will be reconciled to the overa
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank financed
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to stren
  to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sector in part
  is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in accoradance with
  Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read with the
  and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical co
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at
  of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates and d
  drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the assets to th
  under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Capital Work in progre
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed as
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS

  deposited in State Government fund.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO
 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank fin
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to strengthen urba
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation secto
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in acco
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read wi
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historical cost co
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at c
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates a
  duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the asse
  the work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Ca
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entties are folowing a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed ass
  Sstatements, no depreciation is charged.

 2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.2
  deposited in State Government fund.

2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclssified wherever nece
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overal program expenditure will be reconciled to the ove
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO ACCOUN

 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank finan
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to strengthe
  to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sector i
  This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in acco
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank re
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historica
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at c
  of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates and d
  drawbacks and subsidies) and any atributable cost of bringing the assets to
  work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under Capital Work in

  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed a
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03
  deposited in State Government fund.

2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherev
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overal program expenditure will be reconciled t
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Ba
  Program-forResuls (PforR) The objective of the Programis tos
  to climate resiient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation s
  This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Ba
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the his
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.
  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generaly taken under revenue head for the P
  of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of re
  drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the as
  work under this project is on-going, the same was accounted under C
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entties are following a policy of charging depreciation on the
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended
  deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified whereve
2.3 As ths isa PorR, the overll program expenditure willbe recon
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTE
 1, SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
  A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank
  the Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to str
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation s
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepar
  reporting provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the
  Agreement dated 04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05:03.2024.
  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the h
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Pr
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of reba
  of duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the
  Since the work under this project is on-going, the same was accoun
  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fix
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.0
  deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever n
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overal program expenditure wil be reconc
  IBRD and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Prog

         Responsibities of Management and those charged with Governance for the P
         Financial Statements

               The Management of the implementing agency is responsible for the p
         fair presentation of the Program Financial Statements in "accordance with the fina
         reporting framework described in Note 1 to these financial statements, and for such
         control as management determines is necessary to enable the preparation of Progr
         Financial Statements that are free from material misstatement, whether due to fr
               The Management and those. charged with governance are responsible fo
         overseeing the implementing agency's financial reporting process.
         Auditor's Responsibilities for the Audit of the Program Financial Statements
              Qur objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the P
         Financial Statements as a whole are free from material misstatement, whether d
         Or error, and to issue an auditor's report that includes our opinion. Reasona
         high level of assurance but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in acco
         the Standards of Auditing will always detect a material misstatement when "it e
         Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually
         the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisio
         Users taken on the basis of these financial statements.
            As part of an audit in accordance with Standard of Auditing, we exercise professi
         judgment and maintain professional skepticism throughout the audit. We also:
                Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the Program Financial
                 Statements, whether due to fraud or error, design and perform audit proc
                 responsive to those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and
                  appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detectin
                  misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from error, as
                  fraud may involve collusion, forgery, infentional omissions, misrepresent
                  the override of internal control.
                  Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit in order to
                  design audit procedures that are appropriate in the circumstances.
                  Evaluate, the appropriateness 'of "accounting.policies.used and. the
                  reasonableness of "accounting estimates and related disclosures made by
                  management.
                  Evaluate the overall presentation, structure and content of the Program Fina
                  Statements, including the disclosures and whether these financial state
                  present the Project's operations and underlying transactions _and eve
                  manner that achieves fair presentation in accordance with the financial
                  provisions described in Note 1 to the Program Financial Statements.
                  Communicate with those charged with governance regarding, amon
                  matters, the planned scope and timing of the audit and significant audit
                  including any significant deficiencies in internal control that we identify d
                  audit.

SCHEDULE OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND NOTES TO ACCOUN

1, -SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   A. BACKGROUND & PURPOSE
  The Government of Tamil Nadu (GoTN) is implementing the World Bank financed Pro
  Program-for-Results (PforR). The objective of the Program is to stren
  access to climate resilient urban services in Water Supply and Sanitation sector in
  Nadu. This is a special purpose program financial statement prepared in accoradance w
  provisions of Section 5.09 of the General Conditions of the World Bank read wi
  04.03.2024 and Loan Agreement dated 05.03.2024.

  B. CONVENTION
  The financial statements of the Program are prepared under the historic
  generally accepted accounting principles, on cash basis.

  C. FIXED ASSETS
  The Fixed Assets are generally taken under revenue head for the Program at cos
  A/c of respective agencies, which comprises of purchase price (net of rebates an
  duty drawbacks and subsidies) and any attributable cost of bringing the assets to
  the work under this project is on-going. the same was accounted under Capital Work

  D. DEPRECIATION
  The entities are following a policy of charging depreciation on the fixed assets. For th
  statements, no depreciation is charged.

2, NOTES TO ACCOUNTS
2.1 Interest received on Program funds upto the financial year ended 31.03.2025 ag
  deposited in State Government fund.
2.2 Previous Year figures have been regrouped and reclassified wherever necessary.
2.3 As this is a PforR, the overall program expenditure will be reconciled to the over
  and adjustments, if any will be carried out in the last year of the Program.
